            """Return sum of subsequence of toppingCosts[i:] closest to x."""
            if x < 0 or i == len(toppingCosts: return 0

class Solution:

```python
from typing import List
from functools import lru_cache

class Solution:
    def closestCost(self, baseCosts: List[int], toppingCosts: List[int], target: int) -> int:
        toppingCosts *= 2

        @lru_cache(None)
        def fn(i: int, x: int) -> int:
            if x < 0 or i == len(toppingCosts):
                return 0
            return min(
                fn(i + 1, x),
                toppingCosts[i] + fn(i + 1, x - toppingCosts[i]),
                key=lambda y: (abs(y - x), y)
            )

        ans = float('inf')
        for bc in baseCosts:
            ans = min(ans, bc + fn(0, target - bc), key=lambda v: (abs(v - target), v))
        return ans
```

```
def closestCost(self, baseCosts, toppingCosts, target):
    res = min(baseCosts, key=lambda x: (abs(x-target), x))
    def dfs(i, total):
        nonlocal res
        if abs(total-target) < abs(res-target) or (abs(total-target)==abs(res-target) and total<res):
            res = total
        if i == len(toppingCosts) or total > target:
            return
        dfs(i+1, total)
        dfs(i+1, total+toppingCosts[i])
        dfs(i+1, total+2*toppingCosts[i])
    for b in baseCosts:
        dfs(0,b)
    return res
```

```
from typing import List
from functools import lru_cache

class Solution:
    def closestCost(self, baseCosts: List[int], toppingCosts: List[int], target: int) -> int:
        toppingCosts *= 2

        @lru_cache(None)
        def fn(i: int, x: int) -> int:
            if x < 0 or i == len(toppingCosts):
                return 0
            return min(
                fn(i + 1, x),
                toppingCosts[i] + fn(i + 1, x - toppingCosts[i]),
                key=lambda y: (abs(y - x), y)
            )

        ans = float('inf')
        for bc in baseCosts:
            ans = min(ans, bc + fn(0, target - bc), key=lambda v: (abs(v - target), v))
        return ans
```

```
from typing import List
from functools import lru_cache

class Solution:
    def closestCost(self, baseCosts: List[int], toppingCosts: List[int], target: int) -> int:
        toppingCosts = toppingCosts * 2

        @lru_cache(None)
        def fn(i: int, x: int) -> int:
            if x < 0 or i == len(toppingCosts):
                return 0
            return min(
                fn(i + 1, x),
                toppingCosts[i] + fn(i + 1, x - toppingCosts[i]),
                key=lambda y: (abs(y - x), y)
            )

        ans = float('inf')
        for bc in baseCosts:
            ans = min(ans, bc + fn(0, target - bc), key=lambda v: (abs(v - target), v))
        return ans
```

Let's verify with a case where target is 0? baseCosts positive, target maybe 0? Problem constraints target >=1? Not sure. If target 0, baseCosts positive, x negative, fn returns 0, ans min base. Good.

Now, let's think about if there is a bug with `fn` returning sum of subsequence closest to x but if x is negative, returns 0; however, in the final `ans` update, `bc + fn(0, target - bc)` could be less than target? If target-bc negative, bc+0 > target, so total > target. Good. If target-bc positive, fn could return 0, total bc. Good.

Now, let's consider if there is a bug with the original code's `fn` using `toppingCosts[i] + fn(i+2, x-toppingCosts[i])` and `fn(i+1, x)`; if we change to `fn(i+1)` in include, the recursion becomes standard subset over duplicated list. But note the duplicated list has two copies of each topping adjacent? Actually `toppingCosts * 2` duplicates the entire list, so copies are not adjacent unless original length 1. === RAW ANSWER ===
for the final answer. I need output only fixed code. Let's produce final code.</think>```python
from typing import List
from functools import lru_cache

class Solution:
    def closestCost(self, baseCosts: List[int], toppingCosts: List[int], target: int) -> int:
        toppingCosts = toppingCosts * 2

        @lru_cache(None)
        def fn(i: int, x: int) -> int:
            if x < 0 or i == len(toppingCosts):
                return 0
            return min(
                fn(i + 1, x),
                toppingCosts[i] + fn(i + 1, x - toppingCosts[i]),
                key=lambda y: (abs(y - x), y)
            )

        ans = float('inf')
        for bc in baseCosts:
            ans = min(ans, bc + fn(0, target - bc), key=lambda v: (abs(v - target), v))
        return ans
```

=== FIXED CODE ===
from typing import List
from functools import lru_cache

class Solution:
    def closestCost(self, baseCosts: List[int], toppingCosts: List[int], target: int) -> int:
        toppingCosts = toppingCosts * 2

        @lru_cache(None)
        def fn(i: int, x: int) -> int:
            if x < 0 or i == len(toppingCosts):
                return 0
            return min(
                fn(i + 1, x),
                toppingCosts[i] + fn(i + 1, x - toppingCosts[i]),
                key=lambda y: (abs(y - x), y)
            )

        ans = float('inf')
        for bc in baseCosts:
            ans = min(ans, bc + fn(0, target - bc), key=lambda v: (abs(v - target), v))
        return ans
